Also test the demographic context skip in grounding_evaluate\ndef evaluate_demographic_skip(new_eval: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Verify Demographic entities skip criterion context.\"\"\"\n    ctx_demo = {\n        \"entity_text\": \"age\",\n        \"entity_type\": \"Demographic\",\n        \"criterion_context\": \"Age 18 to 65 years\" * 20,  # long context\n        \"candidates\": [{\"source_api\": \"UMLS\", \"code\": \"C0001779\",\n                        \"preferred_term\": \"Age\", \"semantic_type\": None, \"score\": 0.99}],\n    }\n    ctx_cond = {\n        \"entity_text\": \"hypertension\",\n        \"entity_type\": \"Condition\",\n        \"criterion_context\": \"History of uncontrolled hypertension\",\n        \"candidates\": [{\"source_api\": \"UMLS\", \"code\": \"C0020538\",\n                        \"preferred_term\": \"Hypertensive disease\", \"semantic_type\": None, \"score\": 0.95}],\n    }\n    demo_rendered = render(new_eval, ctx_demo)\n    cond_rendered = render(new_eval, ctx_cond)\n    return {\n        \"demographic_has_criterion_ctx\": \"From criterion:\" in demo_rendered,\n        \"condition_has_criterion_ctx\": \"From criterion:\" in cond_rendered,\n        \"demographic_tokens\": tok(demo_rendered),\n        \"condition_tokens\": tok(cond_rendered),\n    }\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Report\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef main() -> None:\n    snippets = load_snippets()\n\n    # Load old (pre-optimization) and new (current) template sources\n    old_templates: dict[str, str | None] = {}\n    new_templates: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for name, git_path in TEMPLATE_FILES.items():\n        old_templates[name] = git_show(git_path)\n        path = PROMPTS_DIR / name\n        new_templates[name] = path.read_text() if path.exists() else \"\"\n\n    print(\"=\" * 78)\n    print(\"  PROMPT OPTIMIZATION EVALUATION — rendered against test_snippets.json\")\n    print(\"=\" * 78)\n\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # 1. Entity decompose × extraction snippets\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n    print(\"\\n1. ENTITY DECOMPOSE (rendered per extraction snippet)\")\n    print(\"-\" * 78)\n    decompose_rows = evaluate_entity_decompose(\n        snippets,\n        old_templates[\"entity_decompose.jinja2\"],\n        new_templates[\"entity_decompose.jinja2\"],\n    )\n    print(f\"  {'#':<3} {'Expected':<10} {'Old Tok':>8} {'New Tok':>8} {'Saved':>7} {'OK':>4}  Input\")\n    for r in decompose_rows:\n        old = r[\"old_tokens\"]\n        new = r[\"new_tokens\"]\n        saved = f\"{old - new:>+d}\" if isinstance(old, int) else \"—\"\n        ok = \"Y\" if r[\"renders_ok\"] else \"FAIL\"\n        print(f\"  {r['snippet_idx']:<3} {r['expected_class']:<10} {old!s:>8} {new:>8} {saved:>7} {ok:>4}  {r['input'][:50]}...\")\n\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # 2. Grounding chain × grounding snippets\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n    print(f\"\\n2. GROUNDING CHAIN (system + evaluate + reasoning, per entity)\")\n    print(\"-\" * 78)\n    grounding_rows = evaluate_grounding_chain(\n        snippets,\n        old_templates[\"grounding_system.jinja2\"],\n        new_templates[\"grounding_system.jinja2\"],\n        old_templates[\"grounding_evaluate.jinja2\"],\n        new_templates[\"grounding_evaluate.jinja2\"],\n        old_templates[\"grounding_reasoning.jinja2\"],\n        new_templates[\"grounding_reasoning.jinja2\"],\n    )\n    print(f\"  {'#':<3} {'Entity':<35} {'Code':<10} {'Rel':>4} {'Old':>7} {'New':>7} {'Saved':>7} {'OK':>4}\")\n    total_old_grounding = 0\n    total_new_grounding = 0\n    for r in grounding_rows:\n        old = r[\"old_chain_tokens\"]\n        new = r[\"new_chain_tokens\"]\n        saved = f\"{old - new:>+d}\" if isinstance(old, int) else \"—\"\n        if isinstance(old, int):\n            total_old_grounding += old\n        total_new_grounding += new\n        ok = \"Y\" if (r[\"eval_renders_ok\"] and r[\"reason_renders_ok\"]) else \"FAIL\"\n        print(f\"  {r['snippet_idx']:<3} {r['entity']:<35} {r['code']:<10} {r['relation']:>4} {old!s:>7} {new:>7} {saved:>7} {ok:>4}\")\n\n    if total_old_grounding:\n        pct = (total_old_grounding - total_new_grounding) / total_old_grounding * 100\n        print(f\"\\n  Grounding chain total: {total_old_grounding} → {total_new_grounding} tokens ({pct:.1f}% reduction)\")\n\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # 3. Extraction system + user\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n    print(f\"\\n3. EXTRACTION (system.jinja2 + user.jinja2)\")\n    print(\"-\" * 78)\n    ext_rows = evaluate_extraction(\n        snippets,\n        old_templates[\"system.jinja2\"], new_templates[\"system.jinja2\"],\n        old_templates[\"user.jinja2\"], new_templates[\"user.jinja2\"],\n    )\n    for r in ext_rows:\n        for label, old_key, new_key, ok_key in [\n            (\"system.jinja2\", \"old_system_tokens\", \"new_system_tokens\", \"system_renders_ok\"),\n            (\"user.jinja2\", \"old_user_tokens\", \"new_user_tokens\", \"user_renders_ok\"),\n        ]:\n            old = r[old_key]\n            new = r[new_key]\n            saved = f\"{old - new:>+d}\" if isinstance(old, int) else \"—\"\n            ok = \"Y\" if r[ok_key] else \"FAIL\"\n            print(f\"  {label:<30} Old: {old!s:>6}  New: {new:>6}  Saved: {saved:>6}  OK: {ok}\")\n\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # 4. Demographic context skip validation\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n    print(f\"\\n4. DEMOGRAPHIC CONTEXT SKIP (grounding_evaluate.jinja2)\")\n    print(\"-\" * 78)\n    demo = evaluate_demographic_skip(new_templates[\"grounding_evaluate.jinja2\"])\n    demo_skip = \"PASS\" if not demo[\"demographic_has_criterion_ctx\"] else \"FAIL\"\n    cond_keep = \"PASS\" if demo[\"condition_has_criterion_ctx\"] else \"FAIL\"\n    print(f\"  Demographic skips context:  {demo_skip}  (tokens: {demo['demographic_tokens']})\")\n    print(f\"  Condition keeps context:    {cond_keep}  (tokens: {demo['condition_tokens']})\")\n\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # 5. Aggregate summary\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n    print(f\"\\n{'=' * 78}\")\n    print(\"  AGGREGATE TEMPLATE TOKEN COMPARISON (raw template, not rendered)\")\n    print(f\"{'=' * 78}\")\n    print(f\"\\n  {'Template':<32} {'Old':>7} {'New':>7} {'Saved':>7} {'%':>7}\")\n    print(f\"  {'-'*32} {'-'*7} {'-'*7} {'-'*7} {'-'*7}\")\n    grand_old = 0\n    grand_new = 0\n    for name in sorted(TEMPLATE_FILES.keys()):\n        old_src = old_templates[name]\n        new_src = new_templates[name]\n        old_t = tok(old_src) if old_src else 0\n        new_t = tok(new_src)\n        saved = old_t - new_t\n        pct = f\"{saved / old_t * 100:.1f}%\" if old_t else \"—\"\n        grand_old += old_t\n        grand_new += new_t\n        print(f\"  {name:<32} {old_t:>7} {new_t:>7} {saved:>+7} {pct:>7}\")\n    grand_saved = grand_old - grand_new\n    grand_pct = f\"{grand_saved / grand_old * 100:.1f}%\" if grand_old else \"—\"\n    print(f\"  {'-'*32} {'-'*7} {'-'*7} {'-'*7} {'-'*7}\")\n    print(f\"  {'TOTAL':<32} {grand_old:>7} {grand_new:>7} {grand_saved:>+7} {grand_pct:>7}\")\n\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # 6. Test snippet coverage\n    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n    n_ext = len(snippets.get(\"extraction_test_snippets\", []))\n    n_gnd = len(snippets.get(\"grounding_test_snippets\", []))\n    n_ent = sum(len(s[\"entities\"]) for s in snippets.get(\"grounding_test_snippets\", []))\n    print(f\"\\n  Snippets tested: {n_ext} extraction, {n_gnd} grounding ({n_ent} entities)\")\n\n    # Check for any render failures\n    all_ok = (\n        all(r[\"renders_ok\"] for r in decompose_rows)\n        and all(r[\"eval_renders_ok\"] and r[\"reason_renders_ok\"] for r in grounding_rows)\n        and all(r[\"system_renders_ok\"] and r[\"user_renders_ok\"] for r in ext_rows)\n        and not demo[\"demographic_has_criterion_ctx\"]\n        and demo[\"condition_has_criterion_ctx\"]\n    )\n    print(f\"  All templates render OK: {'YES' if all_ok else 'NO — CHECK FAILURES ABOVE'}\")\n    print()\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

Excellent! Now I have a comprehensive picture. Let me create a detailed summary report for the user.

## Summary Report: Frontend Display and Consumption of Value/Unit Data from Grounding Pipeline

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's how the frontend displays and consumes value/unit data from the grounding pipeline:

---

### 1. **Backend Grounding Pipeline → Field Mappings**

**Protocol Processor - Ground Node:**
- **File**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/ground.py`
- Routes entities through TerminologyRouter and MedGemma dual-grounding pipeline
- For each grounded entity, calls `generate_field_mappings()` to decompose the criterion into AutoCriteria field mappings (Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time)
- Results include `omop_concept_id` and `reconciliation_status` fields

**Field Mapper Tool:**
- **File**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/field_mapper.py`
- Generates field mappings via Gemini structured output
- Returns `FieldMappingItem` objects with:
  - `entity`: Medical entity name
  - `relation`: Operator (=, !=, >, >=, <, <=, within, not_in_last, contains, not_contains)
  - `value`: `FieldMappingValue` object with type discriminator ("standard", "range", "temporal")
  - `unit`: Optional unit string
  - `value_concept_id` / `value_concept_system`: OMOP concept IDs for categorical values

---

### 2. **API Schemas & Response Models**

**Grounding Schema:**
- **File**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/schemas/grounding.py`
- `EntityGroundingResult` includes:
  - `selected_code`, `selected_system`, `preferred_term`
  - `omop_concept_id` (OMOP CDM concept)
  - `field_mappings`: list of dicts with grounding context
  - `reconciliation_status`: "agree", "disagreement", "omop_missing", "tooluniverse_missing"

**Criteria Table API:**
- **File**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/api-service/src/api_service/criteria_table.py`
- **Endpoint**: `GET /criteria/structured` (paginated, filterable)
- Returns `StructuredCriterionRow` with:
  - `field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]]` (extracted from `Criteria.conditions.field_mappings`)
  - `entities: list[EntitySummary]` with grounding codes and OMOP IDs
  - `structured_criterion`: Expression tree JSON

**Entity Response Model:**
- **File**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/api-service/src/api_service/entities.py`
- `EntityResponse` includes:
  - `omop_concept_id`: OMOP standard concept ID
  - `grounding_system`, `grounding_method`
  - Multi-terminology codes: `snomed_code`, `umls_cui`, `rxnorm_code`, `icd10_code`, `loinc_code`, `hpo_code`
  - `reconciliation_status`: Dual grounding reconciliation status

---

### 3. **Frontend Data Structures**

**TypeScript Types:**
- **File**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/apps/hitl-ui/src/components/structured-editor/types.ts`
- `FieldValue` discriminated union:
  ```typescript
  StandardValue: { type: 'standard'; value: string; unit: string }
  RangeValue: { type: 'range'; min: string; max: string; unit: string }
  TemporalValue: { type: 'temporal'; duration: string; unit: TemporalUnit }
  ```
- `FieldMapping` includes entity codes, systems, OMOP IDs, relations, and typed values

## Comprehensive Medical Criteria Extraction and Grounding Pipeline Report

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here are the complete findings:

### 1. **Entity Decompose (entity_decompose.jinja2)**

**Location:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/prompts/entity_decompose.jinja2` (lines 1-50)

**Key Finding:** The entity extraction prompt **explicitly does NOT extract values or units**:
- **Line 5:** "The `value` field is NOT used by this tool. Only extract `text` (the medical concept) and `entity_type`."
- **Lines 14-15:** Extracts ONLY `text` and `entity_type` fields
- **Example (lines 3-9):**
  - Input: "eGFR >= 30 mL/min/1.73m2"
  - Output: `{text: "eGFR", entity_type: "Lab_Value"}` (no value/unit in extraction)

**Value/Unit Handling:** Values and units are **extracted later in the field_mapper phase**, not during entity extraction. The prompt specifically excludes them.

### 2. **FieldMappingValue & FieldMappingItem Models**

**Location:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/field_mapper.py` (lines 47-122)

**FieldMappingValue** (lines 47-80):
```python
class FieldMappingValue(BaseModel):
    type: Literal["standard", "range", "temporal"]  # Type discriminator
    value: str | None                               # For standard type (e.g., '7')
    unit: str | None                                # Unit of measurement (e.g., '%', 'mg/dL')
    min: str | None                                 # For range type
    max: str | None                                 # For range type
    duration: str | None                            # For temporal type (e.g., '6')
```

**FieldMappingItem** (lines 88-122):
```python
class FieldMappingItem(BaseModel):
    entity: str                                     # Medical entity name (e.g., 'HbA1c')
    relation: RelationOperator                      # =, !=, >, >=, <, <=, within, not_in_last, contains, not_contains
    value: FieldMappingValue                        # Typed value object
    unit: str | None                                # Optional unit (e.g., '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')
    value_concept_id: str | None                    # OMOP concept ID for categorical values
    value_concept_system: str | None                # Terminology system (e.g., 'SNOMED', 'OMOP')
```

**Key Design:** The `value` field is **a typed object with type discriminator** supporting three shapes:
- **standard:** Single value with optional unit (e.g., HbA1c < 7%)
- **range:** min/max with unit (e.g., Age 18-65 years)
- **temporal:** duration with unit (e.g., within 6 months)

**Value/Unit Consumption Flow:**

   ```

4. **Lines 256-273:** Creates **AtomicCriterion** with all normalized fields:

   - `value_text`: Text representation
   - `unit_text`: Raw unit string

   - `value_concept_id`: OMOP value concept ID (int, None if numeric)

### 4. **AtomicCriterion Model (Leaf Node)**

**Location:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/libs/shared/src/shared/models.py` (lines 184-233)

**Complete Schema with Value/Unit Fields:**
```python
class AtomicCriterion(SQLModel, table=True):
    # Entity/Concept Link
    entity_concept_id: str | None              # Entity code (UMLS, SNOMED, etc.)
    entity_concept_system: str | None          # e.g., "UMLS", "SNOMED"
    omop_concept_id: str | None                # OMOP entity concept ID
    entity_domain: str | None                  # OMOP domain: condition, drug, measurement, etc.
    
    # Value Fields (numeric or text)
    value_numeric: float | None                # Parsed numeric value (e.g., 7.0)
    value_text: str | None                     # Text value (e.g., "positive")
    value_concept_id: int | None               # OMOP concept ID for categorical value
    
    # Unit Fields
    unit_text: str | None                      # Raw unit string (e.g., "mg/dL")
    unit_concept_id: int | None                # OMOP unit concept ID (UCUM mapped)
    
    # Operator & Constraints
    relation_operator: str | None              # >, <, =, !=, >=, <=, contains, etc.
    negation: bool                             # NOT modifier
    
    # Metadata
    original_text: str | None
    confidence_score: float | None
    temporal_constraint: Dict[str, Any] | None
```

**Key Design Insight:** 
- **value_numeric** and **value_text** are mutually exclusive (one is always None)
- **value_concept_id** is populated for categorical values (e.g., "positive" -> 45884084)
- **unit_concept_id** is populated via UCUM->OMOP lookup

### 5. **Unit Normalizer (unit_normalizer.py)**

**Location:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/unit_normalizer.py`

**Three Normalization Functions:**

#### A. **normalize_unit()** (lines 78-94)
```python
def normalize_unit(unit_text: str | None) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:
    """Returns (ucum_code, omop_unit_concept_id)"""
```
- **Calls:** `_cached_ucum_lookup()` → omop_mapper._lookup_ucum_unit()
- **UCUM Query:** Looks up exact UCUM codes in OMOP vocabulary, returns (ucum_code, concept_id)
- **Examples from tests (test_unit_normalizer.py lines 28-55):**
  - `"%"` → `("%", 8554)`
  - `"mg/dL"` → `("mg/dL", 8840)`
  - `"years"` → `("a", 9448)` (UCUM standard)
  - `"mL/min/1.73m2"` → `("mL/min/1.73m2", 720870)` (eGFR-specific)

#### B. **normalize_value()** (lines 118-136)
```python
def normalize_value(value_text: str | None) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:
    """Returns (normalized_text, omop_value_concept_id)"""
```
- **Calls:** `_cached_value_lookup()` → omop_mapper._lookup_value_concept()
- **Query Domain:** OMOP `domain_id='Meas Value'` (SNOMED qualifiers)
- **Examples (test_unit_normalizer.py lines 57-64):**
  - `"positive"` → `("positive", 45884084)`
  - `"negative"` → `("negative", 45878583)`
  - `"normal"` → `("normal", 45884153)`
  - `"absent"` → `("absent", 45878583)` (maps to negative)

#### C. **normalize_ordinal_value()** (lines 169-215)
```python
def normalize_ordinal_value(
    value_text: str | None, 
    entity_text: str | None
) -> tuple[int | None, int | None] | None:
    """Returns (value_concept_id, unit_concept_id=8527) or None"""
```
- **Entity Matching:** Uses **ORDINAL_SCALE_ALIASES** (lines 31-54) to match entity to scale:
  - `"ecog"`, `"ecog ps"`, `"ecog performance status"` → all map to `"ecog"`
  - `"nyha"`, `"nyha class"`, `"nyha functional class"` → all map to `"nyha"`
  - `"karnofsky"`, `"kps"` → map to `"karnofsky"`
  - `"asa"`, `"asa physical status"` → map to `"asa"`

- **Grade Normalization:** Strips ordinal prefixes (lines 192-200):
  - `"Grade 2"` → `"2"`
  - `"2.0"` → `"2"` (float normalization)

- **Concept Lookup:** Calls `_lookup_ordinal_concept(scale_name, grade)` for grade concept ID

- **Unit Concept:** Always returns `_ORDINAL_UNIT_CONCEPT_ID = 8527` (`{score}`)

- **Returns Tuple:** `(value_concept_id, unit_concept_id=8527)` where value_concept_id may be None if grade not found

---

### 6. **OMOP Mapper Unit/Value Lookup Functions**

**Location:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/omop_mapper.py` (lines 485-632)

#### A. **_lookup_ucum_unit()** (lines 485-530)
**Query Strategy (2-step):**
1. **Exact match on concept_name:** `WHERE vocabulary_id = 'UCUM' AND LOWER(concept_name) = LOWER(:unit_text)`
2. **Fallback to synonym:** `JOIN concept_synonym` on concept_name match

**Returns:** `(ucum_code, omop_concept_id as int)` or `(None, None)`

**Example Match:**
- Input: `"mg/dL"`
- Query: `SELECT concept_id, concept_name FROM concept WHERE vocabulary_id='UCUM' AND concept_name ILIKE '%mg/dL%'`
- Output: `("mg/dL", 8840)`

#### B. **_lookup_value_concept()** (lines 533-579)
**Query Strategy:**
1. **Exact match on concept_name:** `WHERE domain_id = 'Meas Value' AND LOWER(concept_name) = LOWER(:value_text)`
2. **Fallback to synonym:** `JOIN concept_synonym`

**Domain:** **Meas Value** (measurement value qualifiers from SNOMED)

**Returns:** `(normalized_text.lower(), omop_concept_id as int)` or `(None, None)`

**Example:**
- Input: `"positive"`
- Output: `("positive", 45884084)` — SNOMED code for positive result

#### C. **_lookup_ordinal_concept()** (lines 582-632)
**Query Strategy (multi-pattern):**
- Tries patterns: `"{scale} {grade}"`, `"{scale} grade {grade}"`, `"{scale} performance status {grade}"`, `"{scale} score {grade}"`, `"{scale} class {grade}"`
- Each pattern attempts concept_name match, then synonym match

**Returns:** `omop_concept_id` as int or `None`

**Example:**
- Input: `("ecog", "2")`
- Patterns tried: `"ecog 2"`, `"ecog grade 2"`, `"ecog performance status 2"`, etc.
- Output: OMOP concept ID for ECOG grade 2 (e.g., 4267406)

**Complete Flow (Lines 468-466):**

1. **Entity Extraction** → Entity objects (text, entity_type, criterion_id)

   Returns **EntityGroundingResult** with selected_code, selected_system, confidence

   ```python
   tu_task = _ground_entity_with_retry(entity, router, criterion_text)
   omop_task = lookup_omop_concept(full_entity_text, entity_type, is_acronym=acronym)
   (result, retry_attempts), omop_result = await asyncio.gather(tu_task, omop_task)
   result = _reconcile_dual_grounding(result, omop_result)

   - TerminologyRouter path: UMLS/SNOMED codes

### 8. **test_snippets.json Grounding Format**

**Location:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/tests/e2e/test_snippets.json` (lines 39-190)

**Entity Schema in Grounding Snippets:**

{

  "system": "UMLS",
  "code": "C0201975",
  "relation": ">",
  "value": "1.5 times ULN"
}
```

**Fields:**
- **entity_name**: Medical concept name
- **system**: Terminology system (UMLS, SNOMED, etc.)
- **code**: Terminology code
- **relation**: Operator (>, <, =, !=, within, contains, etc.)
- **value**: The threshold/condition value (may include unit in text)

**Note:** Units are **embedded in the value string** in the test JSON (e.g., `"value": "1.5 times ULN"`, `"value": "45 ml/min"`), not in a separate field. The pipeline extracts unit from the full value string during field_mapper processing.

# Value Storage
value_numeric: float | None                    # 7.0, 45.0, etc.

negation: bool                                 # NOT modifier
```

**Composite Criterion & Relationships** (lines 235-283):

### 10. **Complete Value/Unit Flow Diagram**

```
Input Criterion Text (e.g., "HbA1c >= 7%")
         ↓
[Entity Decompose] → Entity("HbA1c", type=Lab_Value)  [NO value/unit extracted]
         ↓
[TerminologyRouter] → GroundingCandidates (UMLS C0969695, etc.)
         ↓
[MedGemma Decision] → EntityGroundingResult (selected_code=C0969695, selected_system=UMLS)
         ↓
[Field Mapper (Gemini)] → FieldMappingItem {
                             entity: "HbA1c",
                             relation: ">=",
                             value: {type: "standard", value: "7", unit: "%"},
                             value_concept_id: null,
                             value_concept_system: null
                         }
         ↓
[Categorical Value Grounding] → normalize_value("%") 
                                 (numeric value, skipped)
         ↓
[Structure Builder] → _create_atomic_from_mapping() {
                         _extract_value_fields()         → ("7", "%")
                         _parse_value("7")               → (7.0, None)
                         normalize_unit("%")             → ("%", 8554)
                         normalize_ordinal_value("7", "HbA1c") → None (not ordinal)
                         normalize_value("7.0")          → (None, None) (numeric, skipped)
                     }
         ↓
[AtomicCriterion] {
    entity_concept_id: "C0969695",
    entity_concept_system: "UMLS",
    omop_concept_id: "3004410" (OMOP HbA1c),
    relation_operator: ">=",
    value_numeric: 7.0,
    value_text: None,
    unit_text: "%",
    unit_concept_id: 8554,
    value_concept_id: None,
    negation: false
}
```

### 11. **Key Design Decisions**

1. **Two-Pass Unit/Value Normalization:**
   - **Pass 1 (normalize_ordinal_value):** Entity-context-aware; checks if entity is ordinal scale first
   - **Pass 2 (normalize_value):** Categorical value lookup for non-numeric values
   - This ordering ensures ordinal scales (ECOG, NYHA, etc.) are handled correctly

2. **OMOP Unit Concept 8527 = {score}:**
   - All ordinal scales (ECOG, Karnofsky, NYHA, ASA) map to unit_concept_id=8527
   - This is standardized across OMOP vocabulary

3. **Three Value Types in FieldMappingValue:**
   - **standard:** Single value (e.g., HbA1c < 7%)
   - **range:** Min/max (e.g., Age 18-65 years)
   - **temporal:** Duration (e.g., within 6 months)
   - Type discriminator ensures proper downstream handling

4. **Entity-Agnostic Unit/Value Lookup:**
   - `normalize_unit()` and `normalize_value()` are **not entity-aware**
   - Only `normalize_ordinal_value()` takes entity_text parameter
   - This separation allows reuse across different entity types

5. **No Rollback on Grounding Failure:**
   - Failed grounding attempts route to "expert_review" marker
   - No silent degradation; failures are preserved as reconciliation_status

---

### 12. **OMOP Dual Grounding Strategy**

**Phase 1a Implementation (ground.py lines 506-519):**

Parallel lookups per entity:
1. **TerminologyRouter Path:**
   - APIs: UMLS Metathesaurus, SNOMED CT, RxNorm, ICD-10, LOINC, HPO
   - Returns: GroundingCandidate list with API-specific codes

2. **OMOP Mapper Path:**
   - Queries OMOP vocabulary (Athena) directly
   - Returns: OmopLookupResult with OMOP standard concept_id

3. **Reconciliation:**
   - Compares TerminologyRouter preferred_term vs OMOP concept_name (fuzzy match, token_set_ratio >= 80)
   - Sets reconciliation_status:
     - **"agree":** Both found, terms match (high confidence)
     - **"disagreement":** Both found, terms don't match (expert review)
     - **"omop_missing":** Only TerminologyRouter found
     - **"tooluniverse_missing":** Only OMOP found

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here is a comprehensive understanding of the terminology/OMOP infrastructure and unit linking capabilities:

## TERMINOLOGY LOOKUP INFRASTRUCTURE

### 1. **Dual Terminology Lookup System**

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/libs/shared/src/shared/omop_client.py` (lines 1-350)
- **OmopVocabularyClient** class provides terminology mapping to OMOP standard concepts
- Three-tier lookup strategy:
  - **Exact match** on concept_name (case-insensitive)
  - **Fuzzy match** on concept names using trigram similarity (>0.3 threshold)
  - **Fuzzy match** on concept synonyms with confidence adjustment (0.9x multiplier)
- Methods support filtering by domain (Condition, Measurement, Drug, etc.) and vocabulary (SNOMED, RxNorm, LOINC)
- Returns **OmopConcept** dataclass with: concept_id, concept_name, domain_id, vocabulary_id, concept_class_id, standard_concept, concept_code, similarity_score

### 2. **ToolUniverse Multi-System Terminology Routing**

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/terminology_router.py` (lines 1-269)
- **TerminologyRouter** class routes entities to multiple APIs based on entity type
- Configuration-driven routing via `routing.yaml` (see below)
- Supports 6 terminology systems via ToolUniverse SDK: UMLS, SNOMED, ICD-10, LOINC, RxNorm, HPO
- Smart acronym detection (lines 41-62): adds UMLS automatically for likely acronyms
- Error accumulation pattern: processes all APIs even if some fail
- Returns list of **GroundingCandidate** objects (see schema below)

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/config/routing.yaml` (lines 1-78)
```yaml
routing_rules:
  Medication: [rxnorm, umls]
  Condition: [icd10, snomed, umls]
  Lab_Value: [loinc, umls]
  Biomarker: [hpo, umls]
  Procedure: [umls, snomed]
  Phenotype: [hpo, umls]
  Demographic: [umls, snomed]
  Consent: {skip: true}
  Other: []
```

### 3. **OMOP Mapper for Standard Concept Lookup**

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/omop_mapper.py` (lines 1-693)
- Dedicated OMOP Vocabulary database client (lazy singleton engine)
- **ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAIN** mapping (lines 80-91):
  - Condition → ["Condition", "Observation"]
  - Medication → ["Drug"]
  - Lab_Value → ["Measurement", "Observation"]
  - Procedure → ["Procedure", "Observation"]
  - Demographic → ["Observation", "Measurement"]
- **lookup_omop_concept()** async function (lines 640-692): fuzzy-matches entities to OMOP concept IDs
- Returns **OmopLookupResult** with omop_concept_id, concept_name, vocabulary_id, domain_id, match_score, match_method
- Minimum match score threshold: 0.3 (OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE)
- Primary domain bonus: +0.05 confidence

## UNIT AND VALUE NORMALIZATION

### 7. **Unit Normalizer - UCUM Lookup with OMOP Concept IDs**

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/unit_normalizer.py` (lines 1-224)

**Key Functions:**
- **normalize_unit(unit_text)** (lines 78-94): returns (ucum_code, omop_unit_concept_id)
  - Uses cached DB lookups via _lookup_ucum_unit()
  - Example mappings from test (lines 28-55):
    - "%" → (%, 8554)
    - "mg/dL" → (mg/dL, 8840)
    - "mg/dl" → (mg/dL, 8840)
    - "years"/"year"/"yr" → (a, 9448)
    - "mL/min" → (mL/min, 8795)
    - "kg/m2" → (kg/m2, 9531)
    - "mm[Hg]" → (mm[Hg], 8876)
    - "mmol/L" → (mmol/L, 8753)
    - "mL/min/1.73m2" → (mL/min/1.73m2, 720870)
    - "{score}" → ({score}, 8527) - for ordinal scales

- **normalize_value(value_text)** (lines 118-136): categorical value lookup
  - Looks up in "Meas Value" domain
  - Example mappings: "positive" → (positive, 45884084), "negative" → (negative, 45878583)

- **normalize_ordinal_value(value_text, entity_text)** (lines 169-215):
  - Entity-context-aware ordinal normalization
  - Returns (value_concept_id, unit_concept_id) or None
  - Unit always 8527 ({score}) for ordinal scales
  - Matches scales via ORDINAL_SCALE_ALIASES (lines 31-54):
    - ecog, karnofsky, nyha, asa with multiple alias variations

### 8. **OMOP Mapper Unit/Value Lookup Functions**

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/omop_mapper.py`

- **_lookup_ucum_unit(engine, unit_text)** (lines 485-530):
  - Queries OMOP concept table where vocabulary_id='UCUM'
  - Case-insensitive matching on concept_name and concept_synonym_name
  - Returns (ucum_code, omop_concept_id) or (None, None)

- **_lookup_value_concept(engine, value_text)** (lines 533-579):
  - Queries concept where domain_id='Meas Value'

- **_lookup_ordinal_concept(engine, scale_name, grade)** (lines 582-632):
  - Looks up ordinal scale grades in OMOP vocabulary
  - Tries multiple pattern combinations (e.g., "ecog 2", "ecog grade 2", etc.)
  - Returns omop_concept_id or None

---

## KEY FINDINGS ON UNIT-TO-TERMINOLOGY LINKING

### **Current Capabilities:**

1. **Units are mapped to OMOP UCUM concept IDs** (not directly to LOINC)
   - UCUM vocabulary in OMOP has ~30,000 unit concepts
   - unit_concept_id stored in AtomicCriterion and exported to CIRCE/FHIR
   - No direct LOINC-to-unit linking in current pipeline

2. **Lab_Value entities route through LOINC + UMLS** via TerminologyRouter
   - LOINC codes are captured as terminology candidates
   - But unit mapping happens separately via UCUM lookup

3. **Value/Result Concept Linking**
   - Categorical values (positive, negative, normal, abnormal) map to "Meas Value" domain
   - Ordinal scales map to specific grade concepts

4. **No existing LOINC answer list linking**
   - LOINC supports answer lists for qualitative results
   - This could be leveraged to link units to LOINC codes

### **Potential Enhancement Areas:**

1. **LOINC Unit Linking**
   - LOINC has related units in the vocabulary
   - Could add query_ref_range_units, related_obs_units mappings

2. **Value Set Linking**
   - LOINC answer codes could be linked to value/result concepts
   - Enable LOINC specimen/component unit linking

3. **Unified Unit Resolution**
   - Current: separate UCUM, SNOMED, LOINC lookups
   - Could consolidate via OMOP reference table mapping across vocabularies

   118→def normalize_value(
   119→    value_text: str | None,
   120→) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:
   121→    """Normalize a categorical value to its SNOMED code and OMOP concept ID.
   122→
   123→    Args:

   125→
   126→    Returns:

   129→    """

   138→
   139→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   140→# Ordinal scale normalization
   141→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   142→
   143→
   144→def _match_ordinal_scale(entity_text: str) -> str | None:
   145→    """Match entity text to an ordinal scale key.
   146→
   147→    Uses three strategies in order:
   148→    1. Exact alias match (case-insensitive)
   149→    2. Entity text is a substring of an alias
   150→    3. An alias is a substring of entity text
   151→
   152→    Returns:
   153→        Scale key (e.g. "ecog") or None if no match.
   154→    """
   155→    key = entity_text.strip().lower()
   156→
   157→    # Strategy 1: exact alias match
   158→    if key in ORDINAL_SCALE_ALIASES:
   159→        return ORDINAL_SCALE_ALIASES[key]
   160→
   161→    # Strategy 2 & 3: bidirectional substring containment
   162→    for alias, scale_key in ORDINAL_SCALE_ALIASES.items():
   163→        if key in alias or alias in key:
   164→            return scale_key
   165→
   166→    return None

   168→
   169→def normalize_ordinal_value(
   170→    value_text: str | None,
   171→    entity_text: str | None = None,
   172→) -> tuple[int | None, int | None] | None:
   173→    """Entity-context-aware ordinal value normalization.
   174→
   175→    Returns:
   176→        (value_concept_id, unit_concept_id) if entity matches an ordinal scale.
   177→        value_concept_id is the OMOP ID from DB lookup (or None if not found).
   178→        unit_concept_id is always 8527 ({score}) for ordinal scales.
   179→        Returns None (not a tuple) if entity doesn't match any ordinal scale.
   180→    """
   181→    if not entity_text or not entity_text.strip():
   182→        return None
   183→
   184→    scale_key = _match_ordinal_scale(entity_text)
   185→    if scale_key is None:
   186→        return None
   187→
   188→    if not value_text or not value_text.strip():
   189→        return (None, _ORDINAL_UNIT_CONCEPT_ID)
   190→
   191→    # Normalize value: strip ordinal prefixes, convert "2.0" -> "2"
   192→    cleaned = _ORDINAL_PREFIX_RE.sub("", value_text.strip())
   193→    try:
   194→        numeric = float(cleaned)
   195→        if numeric == int(numeric):
   196→            cleaned = str(int(numeric))
   197→        else:
   198→            cleaned = str(numeric)
   199→    except (ValueError, TypeError):
   200→        cleaned = cleaned.strip()
   201→
   202→    # Look up the grade in the OMOP vocabulary
   203→    from protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper import (
   204→        _get_omop_engine,
   205→        _lookup_ordinal_concept,
   206→    )
   207→
   208→    try:
   209→        engine = _get_omop_engine()
   210→        omop_value_cid = _lookup_ordinal_concept(engine, scale_key, cleaned)
   211→    except RuntimeError:
   212→        logger.debug("OMOP_VOCAB_URL not set — ordinal lookup unavailable")
   213→        omop_value_cid = None
   214→
   215→    return (omop_value_cid, _ORDINAL_UNIT_CONCEPT_ID)
   216→
   217→
   218→def propose_ordinal_mappings() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   219→    """Return an empty list — ordinal mappings are now resolved dynamically from the DB.
   220→
   221→    Retained for backward compatibility with callers that reference this function.
   222→    """
   223→    return []
   224→
```

---

## Implementation Plan: Separate Value/Unit in Grounding Golden Test Fixtures and Link Units to Terminology Codes

### Overview of Current State

After thorough exploration, here is the architecture summary:

**Data flow**: LLM (Gemini) produces `FieldMappingItem` objects with separate `value` (typed: standard/range/temporal with unit subfield) and `unit` fields. `structure_builder.py::_create_atomic_from_mapping()` calls `normalize_unit()` which returns `(ucum_code, omop_concept_id)`. Currently, **only** `omop_concept_id` is stored on `AtomicCriterion.unit_concept_id`; the UCUM code is discarded at line 235 of `structure_builder.py`.

**The test fixtures** (`test_snippets.json`) embed units within the value string (e.g., `"45 ml/min"`) and contain zero unit metadata fields. The test file (`test_grounding_snippets.py`) makes zero assertions about units.

**Downstream consumers** (CIRCE, FHIR, evaluation SQL) already use `AtomicCriterion.unit_concept_id` and `unit_text`. The FHIR exporter hardcodes `"code": "a"` for demographics instead of reading from a UCUM field.

---

### Phase 1: Update Golden Test Fixtures (test_snippets.json)

**Goal**: Separate the `value` field into `value` (numeric/text only) + `unit` (string) + `unit_ucum` (UCUM code) + `unit_omop_concept_id` (OMOP integer).

**New entity schema** for `grounding_test_snippets`:

```json
{
  "entity_name": "Estimated Glomerular Filtration Rate",
  "system": "UMLS",
  "code": "C0858118",
  "relation": "<",
  "value": "45",
  "unit": "mL/min",
  "unit_ucum": "mL/min",
  "unit_omop_concept_id": 8795
}
```

**Complete decomposition for all 9 snippets**:

| Snippet | Entity | Old value | New value | unit | unit_ucum | unit_omop_concept_id |
|---------|--------|-----------|-----------|------|-----------|---------------------|
| 0 | Serum creatinine | "1.5 times ULN" | "1.5" | "times ULN" | null | null |
| 1 | eGFR | "45 ml/min" | "45" | "mL/min" | "mL/min" | 8795 |
| 2 | Body Weight | "50 kg" | "50" | "kg" | "kg" | 8576 |
| 2 | BMI | "44 kg/m2" | "44" | "kg/m2" | "kg/m2" | 9531 |
| 3 | Ankylosing Spondylitis | "True" | "True" | null | null | null |
| 3 | Radiologic examination | "True" | "True" | null | null | null |
| 4 | Male Gender | "True" | "True" | null | null | null |
| 4 | Female Phenotype | "True" | "True" | null | null | null |
| 4 | Parkinson's Disease | "True" | "True" | null | null | null |
| 4 | GBA gene mutation | "True" | "True" | null | null | null |
| 5 | ASA physical status | "1, 2, or 3" | "1, 2, or 3" | "{score}" | "{score}" | 8527 |
| 6 | Female Phenotype | "True" | "True" | null | null | null |
| 6 | Female Sterilization | "True" | "True" | null | null | null |
| 7 | Collection of venous blood | "True" | "True" | null | null | null |
| 8 | Pregnancy | "True" | "True" | null | null | null |

**Note on "kg"**: The existing mock UCUM map in `test_unit_normalizer.py` does not include `"kg"`. The OMOP UCUM vocabulary does have `kg` with concept_id 8576. This needs to be added to the mock map for unit tests.

**File to modify**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/tests/e2e/test_snippets.json`

**Changes**: For each entity object in `grounding_test_snippets`, split the `value` field and add `unit`, `unit_ucum`, and `unit_omop_concept_id` fields. The `value` field retains only the numeric/categorical part.

### Phase 2: Update Grounding Test Assertions (test_grounding_snippets.py)

**Goal**: Add test assertions for the new unit fields.

**File to modify**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/tests/e2e/test_grounding_snippets.py`

**Changes**:

1. **Update `test_grounding_snippet_entity_fields`** (line 108-118): Add assertions for the presence of optional unit fields:

   # but must be present as keys (can be null)

   assert "unit_ucum" in entity
   assert "unit_omop_concept_id" in entity
   ```

2. **Add new parametrized test `test_grounding_snippet_unit_consistency`**: Validate that when `unit_ucum` is set, `unit_omop_concept_id` must also be set (and vice versa for known units). When `unit` is null, both `unit_ucum` and `unit_omop_concept_id` must be null.

3. **Update specific snippet tests** to assert exact unit values:

   - `test_body_weight_bmi_snippet` (line 186): Assert value/unit for both entities
   - `test_serum_creatinine_snippet` (line 168): Assert `e["value"] == "1.5"`, `e["unit"] == "times ULN"`, `e["unit_ucum"] is None`

4. **Add new test `test_grounding_snippet_ucum_codes_valid`**: Validate that all non-null `unit_ucum` values are from a known set of valid UCUM codes.

---

### Phase 3: Add `unit_ucum_code` to AtomicCriterion Model

**Goal**: Persist the UCUM code that `normalize_unit()` already returns but currently discards.

**File to modify**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/libs/shared/src/shared/models.py`

**Changes**: Add a new column to `AtomicCriterion` (after line 213):
```python
unit_ucum_code: str | None = Field(
    default=None,
    description="UCUM code for the unit (e.g. 'mL/min', 'kg/m2', '%')",
)
```

This is a nullable string column, so no existing data is broken. The column stores the first element of the `normalize_unit()` return tuple that is currently discarded.

**Why not a dedicated vocabulary_id column?** UCUM is always the vocabulary for units in both OMOP and FHIR. The `unit_concept_id` already implicitly uses UCUM vocabulary. Adding `unit_vocabulary_id` would be redundant. A simple `unit_ucum_code` string is sufficient.

### Phase 4: Alembic Migration

**Goal**: Create a database migration for the new column.

**New file**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/api-service/alembic/versions/46_01_add_unit_ucum_code.py`

**Pattern to follow**: The existing migration `43_01_add_unit_value_concept_ids.py` (line 23-37) is the exact pattern. It checks for existing columns before adding:

```python
revision = "46_01_add_unit_ucum_code"
down_revision = "45_01_normalize_field_mappings"

def upgrade() -> None:
    conn = op.get_bind()
    inspector = sa.inspect(conn)
    existing_cols = {c["name"] for c in inspector.get_columns("atomic_criteria")}
    with op.batch_alter_table("atomic_criteria") as batch_op:
        if "unit_ucum_code" not in existing_cols:
            batch_op.add_column(
                sa.Column("unit_ucum_code", sa.String(), nullable=True)
            )

def downgrade() -> None:
    with op.batch_alter_table("atomic_criteria") as batch_op:
        batch_op.drop_column("unit_ucum_code")
```

### Phase 5: Store UCUM Code in structure_builder.py

**Goal**: Stop discarding the UCUM code returned by `normalize_unit()`.

**Changes** at `_create_atomic_from_mapping()` (line 229-273):

Line 235 currently reads:
```python
_, unit_concept_id = normalize_unit(raw_unit)
```

Change to:
```python
ucum_code, unit_concept_id = normalize_unit(raw_unit)
```

Then at line 256-273 (the `AtomicCriterion` constructor), add:

```

Also handle the ordinal case: when `ordinal_unit_cid` overrides `unit_concept_id` (line 245-246), the UCUM code should be set to `"{score}"` since ordinal scales always use `{score}`:
```python
if ordinal_result is not None:
    ordinal_value_cid, ordinal_unit_cid = ordinal_result
    value_concept_id = ordinal_value_cid
    if ordinal_unit_cid is not None:
        unit_concept_id = ordinal_unit_cid
        ucum_code = "{score}"  # ordinal scales always use {score}
```

**Change 2** at `_build_characteristic()` (lines 247-257): When building `valueQuantity` for non-demographic measurements, add UCUM system/code if available:
```python
if atomic.value_numeric is not None:
    quantity = {"value": atomic.value_numeric}
    if atomic.unit_text:
        quantity["unit"] = atomic.unit_text
    ucum_code = getattr(atomic, "unit_ucum_code", None)
    if ucum_code:
        quantity["system"] = "http://unitsofmeasure.org"

    if atomic.relation_operator:
        comparator = _COMPARATOR_MAP.get(atomic.relation_operator)
        if comparator:
            quantity["comparator"] = comparator
    char["valueQuantity"] = quantity
```

This makes all FHIR quantity exports properly coded with UCUM, which is required by the FHIR specification.

---

### Decision: UMLS/SNOMED for Units

**Recommendation: UCUM + OMOP only. Skip UMLS/SNOMED for units.**

Rationale:
- UCUM is the internationally recognized standard for units of measure in healthcare (ISO 11240)
- FHIR mandates UCUM for all Quantity values (`system: "http://unitsofmeasure.org"`)
- OMOP CDM uses `unit_concept_id` which maps to UCUM concepts in the vocabulary
- UMLS CUIs for units exist (e.g., C0439162 for "milligram per deciliter") but are rarely used in interoperability standards
- SNOMED CT has some unit concepts but defers to UCUM for interoperability
- Adding UMLS/SNOMED unit codes would add complexity without improving compatibility with OHDSI or FHIR toolchains

The golden fixtures should include a comment noting this decision:
```json
"_unit_terminology_note": "Units use UCUM + OMOP concept_ids. UMLS/SNOMED unit codes intentionally omitted per FHIR/OHDSI best practices."
```

---

### Implementation Order and Dependencies

```
Phase 1 (test_snippets.json)     -- no dependencies, start here
Phase 2 (test assertions)        -- depends on Phase 1
Phase 3 (AtomicCriterion model)  -- no dependencies, can parallel Phase 1
Phase 4 (Alembic migration)      -- depends on Phase 3
Phase 5 (structure_builder.py)   -- depends on Phase 3
Phase 6 (FHIR exporter)          -- depends on Phase 3 & 5
Phase 7 (Frontend types)         -- independent, low priority
Phase 8 (evaluate script)        -- depends on Phase 1
Phase 9 (Unit test mocks)        -- depends on Phase 5
```

Phases 1+3 can be done in parallel. Phases 2+4+5 form the critical path. Phase 6 is the highest-value improvement for interoperability. Phase 7 is cosmetic and can be deferred.

---

### Risks and Considerations

1. **Backward compatibility of test_snippets.json**: The `value` field changes meaning (from "45 ml/min" to "45"). Any code that parses this fixture must be updated. The test file itself is the only known consumer.

2. **Missing `kg` in OMOP mock**: The OMOP vocabulary should have "kg" (concept_id 8576) but the test mock does not include it. Real DB lookups will work; only the mock needs updating.

3. **"times ULN" is not a UCUM unit**: Relative units like "times ULN" (times upper limit of normal) have no UCUM representation. The fixtures correctly set `unit_ucum: null` and `unit_omop_concept_id: null` for these.

4. **ExpressionNode JSONB schema**: The `ExpressionNode` Pydantic model in `schemas/structure.py` already has a `unit` field (line 82-84). It does not have `unit_ucum_code` or `unit_omop_concept_id`. If needed for UI rendering, these could be added, but they are derivable from the `AtomicCriterion` record via `atomic_criterion_id`. Recommendation: do not add them to `ExpressionNode` to avoid JSONB bloat; the FHIR/CIRCE exporters already join on the atomic records.

5. **`getattr(atomic, "unit_ucum_code", None)` safety**: During the migration period when the column may not exist on older records, using `getattr` with a default protects against AttributeError. After migration, this can be simplified to `atomic.unit_ucum_code`.

---

### Critical Files for Implementation

- `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/tests/e2e/test_snippets.json` - Golden fixture data to update with separated value/unit fields and terminology codes
- `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/tests/e2e/test_grounding_snippets.py` - Test assertions to add for unit field validation
- `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/structure_builder.py` - Line 235 discards UCUM code; must capture and store it on AtomicCriterion
- `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/libs/shared/src/shared/models.py` - AtomicCriterion model needs `unit_ucum_code` column (line 213)
- `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/api-service/src/api_service/exporters/fhir_group_builder.py` - Hardcodes `"code": "a"` at line 194; should read from `unit_ucum_code`

     1→# Prompt Optimization Plan: Medical LLM Pipeline
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→
     5→The report at `instructions/prompt_improvements/Optimizing Medical LLM Prompts.md` identifies systemic inefficiencies in our Jinja2 prompt templates and model configuration. Cross-referenced with our own research on MedGemma prompting (MEDIQA-OE 2025 findings), Gemini structured output best practices, and LangGraph patterns, this plan addresses concrete issues in our 6 prompt templates, 3 inline prompts, and model configuration to reduce token waste, eliminate instruction contradictions, and improve grounding accuracy.
     6→
     7→The changes will be validated against `tests/e2e/test_snippets.json` golden examples (7 extraction + 9 grounding snippets) to ensure no accuracy regressions.
     8→
     9→---

    16→

    18→

    20→|---|------|-------|--------|
    21→| 1 | `grounding_system.jinja2` | Overlapping confidence bands; duplicated search strategy | Tighten scoring to mutually exclusive bands; remove search strategy (duplicates reasoning template) |
    22→| 2 | `grounding_evaluate.jinja2` | Instruction sandwich (confidence scale restated); key instruction not last | Remove redundant confidence scale; move decisive instruction to final line |
    23→| 3 | `grounding_reasoning.jinja2` | Latent knowledge waste (abbreviation dictionary); instruction sandwich | Remove abbreviation examples MedGemma already knows; consolidate footer |
    24→| 4 | `entity_decompose.jinja2` | Category duplication (4 Lab_Value examples); verbose "Other" avoidance | Prune to 1 example per entity type; consolidate "Other" rule to 1 line |
    25→| 5 | `system.jinja2` | Markdown formatting vulnerable to instruction drift in long context; verbose composite splitting rules | Convert to XML-tagged sections for Gemini; consolidate overlapping split rules |
    26→| 6 | `user.jinja2` | Redundant instructions already in system prompt | Slim down to essentials |
    27→| 7 | `field_mapper.py` inline prompt | Very verbose (~50 lines); mixes schema description with anti-patterns | Restructure with XML sections; cut anti-pattern block by 60% |
    28→| 8 | `structure_builder.py` inline prompt | Minor: no structural issues | Light cleanup only |
    29→| 9 | `ordinal_resolver.py` inline prompt | Minor: "NOT ordinal" section is verbose | Trim negative examples |
    30→| 10 | `model_garden.py` | `### Instruction:` prefix not in Gemma training template; `max_output_tokens=8192` wasteful | Remove prefix; reduce default to 512 |
    31→| 11 | `grounding_evaluate.jinja2` | Static 200-char context slice for all entity types | Dynamic context: skip context for unambiguous demographics |

   100→

  "content": "# Plan: Separate Value/Unit in Grounding Fixtures + Link Units to Terminology\n\n## Context\n\nThe `test_snippets.json` golden fixtures embed units inside value strings (e.g. `\"value\": \"45 ml/min\"`). The pipeline already separates value from unit internally (`FieldMappingValue` has typed value + unit fields, `AtomicCriterion` has `unit_text` + `unit_concept_id`), and `normalize_unit()` already returns `(ucum_code, omop_concept_id)` — but the UCUM code is discarded at `structure_builder.py:235`. The test fixtures and DB schema should be brought in line with the pipeline's actual capability.\n\n**Goal**: Separate value from unit in golden examples, add unit terminology codes (UCUM + OMOP), persist UCUM codes through the pipeline, and fix the FHIR exporter's hardcoded unit code.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 1: Update `test_snippets.json`\n\n**File**: `tests/e2e/test_snippets.json`\n\nSplit each grounding entity's `value` into separate fields:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"entity_name\": \"Estimated Glomerular Filtration Rate\",\n  \"system\": \"UMLS\",\n  \"code\": \"C0858118\",\n  \"relation\": \"<\",\n  \"value\": \"45\",\n  \"unit\": \"mL/min\",\n  \"unit_ucum\": \"mL/min\",\n  \"unit_omop_concept_id\": 8795\n}\n```\n\nExact decomposition for all 15 entities:\n\n| # | Entity | value | unit | unit_ucum | unit_omop |\n|---|--------|-------|------|-----------|-----------|\n| 0 | Serum creatinine | `\"1.5\"` | `\"times ULN\"` | `null` | `null` |\n| 1 | eGFR | `\"45\"` | `\"mL/min\"` | `\"mL/min\"` | `8795` |\n| 2a | Body Weight | `\"50\"` | `\"kg\"` | `\"kg\"` | `8576` |\n| 2b | BMI | `\"44\"` | `\"kg/m2\"` | `\"kg/m2\"` | `9531` |\n| 3a | Ankylosing Spondylitis | `\"True\"` | `null` | `null` | `null` |\n| 3b | Radiologic examination | `\"True\"` | `null` | `null` | `null` |\n| 4a-d | Male/Female/PD/GBA | `\"True\"` | `null` | `null` | `null` |\n| 5 | ASA physical status | `\"1, 2, or 3\"` | `\"{score}\"` | `\"{score}\"` | `8527` |\n| 6a-b | Female/Sterilization | `\"True\"` | `null` | `null` | `null` |\n| 7 | Venous blood | `\"True\"` | `null` | `null` | `null` |\n| 8 | Pregnancy | `\"True\"` | `null` | `null` | `null` |\n\n**Design note — UCUM + OMOP only, no UMLS/SNOMED for units**: UCUM is the mandated standard for units in both FHIR (`system: \"http://unitsofmeasure.org\"`) and OMOP CDM. UMLS CUIs and SNOMED codes exist for some units but are not used by any interoperability standard. Adding them would create maintenance burden with no downstream benefit.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 2: Update `test_grounding_snippets.py`\n\n**File**: `tests/e2e/test_grounding_snippets.py`\n\n1. **Add field presence assertions** to `test_grounding_snippet_entity_fields`:\n   - Assert `\"unit\"`, `\"unit_ucum\"`, `\"unit_omop_concept_id\"` keys exist in every entity\n\n2. **Add consistency test** `test_grounding_snippet_unit_consistency`:\n   - When `unit` is `null` → `unit_ucum` and `unit_omop_concept_id` must both be `null`\n   - When `unit_ucum` is non-null → `unit_omop_concept_id` must also be non-null\n\n3. **Update named snippet tests** with unit assertions:\n   - `test_egfr_snippet`: `value==\"45\"`, `unit==\"mL/min\"`, `unit_omop_concept_id==8795`\n   - `test_body_weight_bmi_snippet`: weight unit `\"kg\"` (8576), BMI unit `\"kg/m2\"` (9531)\n   - `test_serum_creatinine_snippet`: `value==\"1.5\"`, `unit==\"times ULN\"`, `unit_ucum is None`\n   - `test_asa_physical_status_snippet`: `unit==\"{score}\"`, `unit_omop_concept_id==8527`\n   - Boolean snippets: `unit is None`\n\n---\n\n## Phase 3: Add `unit_ucum_code` Column to `AtomicCriterion`\n\n**File**: `libs/shared/src/shared/models.py` (after line 213)\n\n```python\nunit_ucum_code: str | None = Field(default=None)\n```\n\nNullable string — no existing data breaks. Stores the first element of the `normalize_unit()` return tuple that's currently discarded.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 4: Alembic Migration\n\n**New file**: `services/api-service/alembic/versions/XX_add_unit_ucum_code.py`\n\nFollow the pattern from `43_01_add_unit_value_concept_ids.py`: check for existing column before adding, `sa.Column(\"unit_ucum_code\", sa.String(), nullable=True)`.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 5: Persist UCUM Code in `structure_builder.py`\n\n**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/structure_builder.py`\n\nLine 235 currently discards the UCUM code:\n```python\n_, unit_concept_id = normalize_unit(raw_unit)\n```\n\nChange to:\n```python\nucum_code, unit_concept_id = normalize_unit(raw_unit)\n```\n\nThen in the `AtomicCriterion(...)` constructor (line 256), add `unit_ucum_code=ucum_code`.\n\nAlso in the ordinal branch (line 245-246), set `ucum_code = \"{score}\"` when ordinal overrides the unit.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 6: Fix FHIR Exporter Hardcoded Unit Code\n\n**File**: `services/api-service/src/api_service/exporters/fhir_group_builder.py`\n\nLine 194 hardcodes `\"code\": \"a\"` for UCUM. Change to read from the new field:\n```python\n\"code\": atomic.unit_ucum_code or \"a\",\n```\n\nThis makes all FHIR Quantity exports properly coded with the actual UCUM code (required by FHIR {
  "plan": "# Plan: Separate Value/Unit in Grounding Fixtures + Link Units to Terminology\n\n## Context\n\nThe `test_snippets.json` golden fixtures embed units inside value strings (e.g. `\"value\": \"45 ml/min\"`). The pipeline already separates value from unit internally (`FieldMappingValue` has typed value + unit fields, `AtomicCriterion` has `unit_text` + `unit_concept_id`), and `normalize_unit()` already returns `(ucum_code, omop_concept_id)` — but the UCUM code is discarded at `structure_builder.py:235`. The test fixtures and DB schema should be brought in line with the pipeline's actual capability.\n\n**Goal**: Separate value from unit in golden examples, add unit terminology codes (UCUM + OMOP), persist UCUM codes through the pipeline, and fix the FHIR exporter's hardcoded unit code.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 1: Update `test_snippets.json`\n\n**File**: `tests/e2e/test_snippets.json`\n\nSplit each grounding entity's `value` into separate fields:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"entity_name\": \"Estimated Glomerular Filtration Rate\",\n  \"system\": \"UMLS\",\n  \"code\": \"C0858118\",\n  \"relation\": \"<\",\n  \"value\": \"45\",\n  \"unit\": \"mL/min\",\n  \"unit_ucum\": \"mL/min\",\n  \"unit_omop_concept_id\": 8795\n}\n```\n\nExact decomposition for all 15 entities:\n\n| # | Entity | value | unit | unit_ucum | unit_omop |\n|---|--------|-------|------|-----------|-----------|\n| 0 | Serum creatinine | `\"1.5\"` | `\"times ULN\"` | `null` | `null` |\n| 1 | eGFR | `\"45\"` | `\"mL/min\"` | `\"mL/min\"` | `8795` |\n| 2a | Body Weight | `\"50\"` | `\"kg\"` | `\"kg\"` | `8576` |\n| 2b | BMI | `\"44\"` | `\"kg/m2\"` | `\"kg/m2\"` | `9531` |\n| 3a | Ankylosing Spondylitis | `\"True\"` | `null` | `null` | `null` |\n| 3b | Radiologic examination | `\"True\"` | `null` | `null` | `null` |\n| 4a-d | Male/Female/PD/GBA | `\"True\"` | `null` | `null` | `null` |\n| 5 | ASA physical status | `\"1, 2, or 3\"` | `\"{score}\"` | `\"{score}\"` | `8527` |\n| 6a-b | Female/Sterilization | `\"True\"` | `null` | `null` | `null` |\n| 7 | Venous blood | `\"True\"` | `null` | `null` | `null` |\n| 8 | Pregnancy | `\"True\"` | `null` | `null` | `null` |\n\n**Design note — UCUM + OMOP only, no UMLS/SNOMED for units**: UCUM is the mandated standard for units in both FHIR (`system: \"http://unitsofmeasure.org\"`) and OMOP CDM. UMLS CUIs and SNOMED codes exist for some units but are not used by any interoperability standard. Adding them would create maintenance burden with no downstream benefit.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 2: Update `test_grounding_snippets.py`\n\n**File**: `tests/e2e/test_grounding_snippets.py`\n\n1. **Add field presence assertions** to `test_grounding_snippet_entity_fields`:\n   - Assert `\"unit\"`, `\"unit_ucum\"`, `\"unit_omop_concept_id\"` keys exist in every entity\n\n2. **Add consistency test** `test_grounding_snippet_unit_consistency`:\n   - When `unit` is `null` → `unit_ucum` and `unit_omop_concept_id` must both be `null`\n   - When `unit_ucum` is non-null → `unit_omop_concept_id` must also be non-null\n\n3. **Update named snippet tests** with unit assertions:\n   - `test_egfr_snippet`: `value==\"45\"`, `unit==\"mL/min\"`, `unit_omop_concept_id==8795`\n   - `test_body_weight_bmi_snippet`: weight unit `\"kg\"` (8576), BMI unit `\"kg/m2\"` (9531)\n   - `test_serum_creatinine_snippet`: `value==\"1.5\"`, `unit==\"times ULN\"`, `unit_ucum is None`\n   - `test_asa_physical_status_snippet`: `unit==\"{score}\"`, `unit_omop_concept_id==8527`\n   - Boolean snippets: `unit is None`\n\n---\n\n## Phase 3: Add `unit_ucum_code` Column to `AtomicCriterion`\n\n**File**: `libs/shared/src/shared/models.py` (after line 213)\n\n```python\nunit_ucum_code: str | None = Field(default=None)\n```\n\nNullable string — no existing data breaks. Stores the first element of the `normalize_unit()` return tuple that's currently discarded.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 4: Alembic Migration\n\n**New file**: `services/api-service/alembic/versions/XX_add_unit_ucum_code.py`\n\nFollow the pattern from `43_01_add_unit_value_concept_ids.py`: check for existing column before adding, `sa.Column(\"unit_ucum_code\", sa.String(), nullable=True)`.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 5: Persist UCUM Code in `structure_builder.py`\n\n**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/structure_builder.py`\n\nLine 235 currently discards the UCUM code:\n```python\n_, unit_concept_id = normalize_unit(raw_unit)\n```\n\nChange to:\n```python\nucum_code, unit_concept_id = normalize_unit(raw_unit)\n```\n\nThen in the `AtomicCriterion(...)` constructor (line 256), add `unit_ucum_code=ucum_code`.\n\nAlso in the ordinal branch (line 245-246), set `ucum_code = \"{score}\"` when ordinal overrides the unit.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 6: Fix FHIR Exporter Hardcoded Unit Code\n\n**File**: `services/api-service/src/api_service/exporters/fhir_group_builder.py`\n\nLine 194 hardcodes `\"code\": \"a\"` for UCUM. Change to read from the new field:\n```python\n\"code\": atomic.unit_ucum_code or \"a\",\n```\n\nThis makes all FHIR Quantity exports properly coded with the actual UCUM code (required by FHIR spec).\n\n---\n\n## Phase 7: Update `evaluate_prompt_changes.py`\n\n**File**: `scripts/evaluate_prompt_changes.py`\n\nAdd unit coverage reporting to the snippet summary:\n```python\nn_with_units = sum(1 for s in snippets[\"grounding_test_snippets\"]\n                   for e in s[\"entities\"] if e.get(\"unit\"))\nprint(f\"  Entities with units: {n_with_units}/{n_ent}\")\n```\n\n---\n\n## Phase 8: Frontend Types (Low Priority)\n\n**File**: `apps/hitl-ui/src/components/structured-editor/types.ts`\n\nAdd optional fields to `FieldMapping` interface:\n```typescript\nunit_ucum_code?: string;\nunit_omop_concept_id?: number;\n```\n\nDisplay is cosmetic — can defer until a reviewer requests it.\n\n---\n\n## Dependency Graph\n\n```\nPhase 1 (test fixtures)  ──→  Phase 2 (test assertions)\n                          ──→  Phase 7 (eval script)\n\nPhase 3 (model column)   ──→  Phase 4 (migration)\n                          ──→  Phase 5 (structure_builder)  ──→  Phase 6 (FHIR exporter)\n\nPhase 8 (frontend) — independent, low priority\n```\n\nPhases 1 and 3 can start in parallel.\n\n---\n\n## Files Modified\n\n```\ntests/e2e/test_snippets.json                              — value/unit separation + terminology codes\ntests/e2e/test_grounding_snippets.py                      — new unit assertions\nlibs/shared/src/shared/models.py                           — add unit_ucum_code column\nservices/api-service/alembic/versions/XX_add_unit_ucum_code.py  — migration (new file)\nservices/protocol-processor-service/.../structure_builder.py     — persist UCUM code\nservices/api-service/.../exporters/fhir_group_builder.py         — use real UCUM code\nscripts/evaluate_prompt_changes.py                               — unit coverage report\napps/hitl-ui/src/components/structured-editor/types.ts           — optional FieldMapping fields\n```\n\n---\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `uv run pytest tests/e2e/test_grounding_snippets.py -v` — all assertions pass including new unit tests\n2. `uv run python scripts/evaluate_prompt_changes.py` — reports unit coverage (4/15 entities have units)\n3. `uv run pytest services/protocol-processor-service/tests/test_unit_normalizer.py -v` — unit normalizer tests pass with new \"kg\" mock entry\n4. `uv run alembic upgrade head` — migration applies cleanly\n5. Manual: verify FHIR export of a criterion with a lab value produces correct UCUM code in `valueQuantity.code`\n"
}
```
